Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The paleobiology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 840 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 250 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to paleobiology structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 840/250 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on paleobiology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 784. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 803 with relations in degree 3.
